#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            n = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrix
    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
    std::vector<double> A_orig;
    
    // Generate positive definite matrix
    printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
    
    if (validate) {
        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation
    }
    
    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
    printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    if (!success) {
        printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            n = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrix
    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
    std::vector<double> A_orig;
    
    // Generate positive definite matrix
    printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
    
    if (validate) {
        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation
    }
    
    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
    printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    if (!success) {
        printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)

+struct ProcessGrid {
+    int rank, size, rows, cols, row, col;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm, colComm;
+
+    ProcessGrid() {
+        MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);
+        int dims[2] = {0, 0};
+        MPI_Dims_create(size, 2, dims);
+        rows = dims[0];
+        cols = dims[1];
+        row = rank / cols;
+        col = rank % cols;
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, row, col, &rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, col, row, &colComm);
+    }
+
+    ~ProcessGrid() {
+        MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&colComm);
+    }
+
+    int owner(size_t i, size_t j) const {
+        return static_cast<int>(i % rows) * cols + static_cast<int>(j % cols);
+    }
+};
+
+struct Tile {
+    size_t i, j, offset;
+};
+
+// Only locally owned tiles are allocated. The factor stores the lower half;
+// the temporary random matrix uses the same layout with all tiles present.
+struct DistributedMatrix {
+    const ProcessGrid& grid;
+    size_t n, blocks, localRows, localCols, area;
+    int block;
+    std::vector<size_t> offsets;
+    std::vector<Tile> tiles;
+    std::vector<double> data;
+
+    DistributedMatrix(const ProcessGrid& g, size_t size, int b, bool lower = true)
+        : grid(g), n(size), blocks(size / b + (size % b != 0)),
+          localRows(blocks / g.rows + (static_cast<size_t>(g.row) < blocks % g.rows)),
+          localCols(blocks / g.cols + (static_cast<size_t>(g.col) < blocks % g.cols)),
+          area(static_cast<size_t>(b) * b), block(b),
+          offsets(localRows * localCols, std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
+        // Panel collectives use MPI's int counts; the matrix itself may exceed
+        // this limit and is transferred in chunks when results are requested.
+        if (std::max(localRows, localCols) > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) / area)
+            throw std::length_error("Matrix panels exceed the MPI count limit");
+        for (size_t i = g.row; i < blocks; i += g.rows) {
+            for (size_t j = g.col; j < blocks && (!lower || j <= i); j += g.cols) {
+                const size_t offset = tiles.size() * area;
+                offsets[(i / g.rows) * localCols + j / g.cols] = offset;
+                tiles.push_back({i, j, offset});
+            }
+        }
+        data.resize(tiles.size() * area, 0.0);
+    }
+
+    const double* tile(size_t i, size_t j) const {
+        const size_t offset = offsets[(i / grid.rows) * localCols + j / grid.cols];
+        return offset == std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() ? nullptr : data.data() + offset;
+    }
+
+    double* tile(size_t i, size_t j) {
+        return const_cast<double*>(static_cast<const DistributedMatrix&>(*this).tile(i, j));
+    }
+
+    int extent(size_t i) const {
+        return static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(block), n - i * block));
+    }
+};
+
+struct PanelWorkspace {
+    std::vector<double> left, right;
+    std::vector<int> counts, displacements;
+    std::vector<size_t> offsets;
+
+    explicit PanelWorkspace(const DistributedMatrix& a)
+        : left(a.localRows * a.area), right(a.localCols * a.area),
+          counts(a.grid.rows), displacements(a.grid.rows), offsets(a.blocks) {}
 
-bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
-    // A is stored in row-major order

-            if (i == j) {
-                // Diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
+    // Broadcast the column panel along grid rows, then redistribute its
+    // transpose within grid columns. Aggregating tiles gives two collectives
+    // per panel, including on nonsquare process grids. The communicated volume
+    // per rank is O(n * block / sqrt(p)) on a balanced grid.
+    void exchange(const DistributedMatrix& a, size_t k, size_t first) {
+        const ProcessGrid& g = a.grid;
+        const size_t firstRow = first / g.rows + (static_cast<size_t>(g.row) < first % g.rows);
+        if (g.col == static_cast<int>(k % g.cols)) {
+            for (size_t r = firstRow; r < a.localRows; ++r) {
+                const double* source = a.tile(r * g.rows + g.row, k);
+                double* target = left.data() + r * a.area;
+                if (source)
+                    std::copy_n(source, a.area, target);
+                else
+                    std::fill_n(target, a.area, 0.0);
+            }
+        }
+        const int count = static_cast<int>((a.localRows - firstRow) * a.area);
+        MPI_Bcast(count ? left.data() + firstRow * a.area : left.data(), count,
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, static_cast<int>(k % g.cols), g.rowComm);
+
+        std::fill(counts.begin(), counts.end(), 0);
+        const size_t firstCol = first / g.cols + (static_cast<size_t>(g.col) < first % g.cols);
+        for (size_t c = firstCol; c < a.localCols; ++c) {
+            const size_t j = c * g.cols + g.col;
+            offsets[j] = counts[j % g.rows];
+            counts[j % g.rows] += static_cast<int>(a.area);
+        }
+        int total = 0;
+        for (int r = 0; r < g.rows; ++r) {
+            displacements[r] = total;
+            total += counts[r];
+        }
+        for (size_t c = firstCol; c < a.localCols; ++c) {
+            const size_t j = c * g.cols + g.col;
+            offsets[j] += displacements[j % g.rows];
+            if (static_cast<int>(j % g.rows) == g.row) {
+                const double* source = left.data() + (j / g.rows) * a.area;
+                double* target = right.data() + offsets[j];
+                for (int i = 0; i < a.block; ++i)
+                    for (int t = 0; t < a.block; ++t)
+                        target[t * a.block + i] = source[i * a.block + t];
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DOUBLE, right.data(), counts.data(),
+                       displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, g.colComm);
+    }
+};

+// A portable 4-by-8 register kernel. SIMD runs across independent output
+// entries, avoiding horizontal reductions or unsafe floating-point flags.
+// add=true preserves the original k-order when generating/reconstructing A.
+template <bool add>
+void tileProduct(double* __restrict target, const double* __restrict left,
+                 const double* __restrict right, int block) {
+    for (int i = 0; i < block; i += 4) {
+        for (int j = 0; j < block; j += 8) {
+            double c0[8], c1[8], c2[8], c3[8];
+            for (int t = 0; t < 8; ++t) {
+                c0[t] = add ? target[(i + 0) * block + j + t] : 0.0;
+                c1[t] = add ? target[(i + 1) * block + j + t] : 0.0;
+                c2[t] = add ? target[(i + 2) * block + j + t] : 0.0;
+                c3[t] = add ? target[(i + 3) * block + j + t] : 0.0;
+            }
+            for (int k = 0; k < block; ++k) {
+                const double a0 = left[(i + 0) * block + k];
+                const double a1 = left[(i + 1) * block + k];
+                const double a2 = left[(i + 2) * block + k];
+                const double a3 = left[(i + 3) * block + k];
+                const double* b = right + k * block + j;
+                for (int t = 0; t < 8; ++t) {
+                    c0[t] += a0 * b[t];
+                    c1[t] += a1 * b[t];
+                    c2[t] += a2 * b[t];
+                    c3[t] += a3 * b[t];
                 }
-                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
-                if (val <= 0.0) {
-                    // Matrix is not positive definite
-                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
-                    return false;
+            }
+            for (int t = 0; t < 8; ++t) {
+                if constexpr (add) {
+                    target[(i + 0) * block + j + t] = c0[t];
+                    target[(i + 1) * block + j + t] = c1[t];
+                    target[(i + 2) * block + j + t] = c2[t];
+                    target[(i + 3) * block + j + t] = c3[t];
+                } else {
+                    target[(i + 0) * block + j + t] -= c0[t];
+                    target[(i + 1) * block + j + t] -= c1[t];
+                    target[(i + 2) * block + j + t] -= c2[t];
+                    target[(i + 3) * block + j + t] -= c3[t];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}

+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(DistributedMatrix& a, PanelWorkspace& panels) {
+    DistributedMatrix random(a.grid, a.n, a.block, false);
+    std::vector<double> slab, packed;
+    if (a.grid.rank == 0) {
+        // Stream the exact original rand_r sequence without a replicated n^2
+        // input allocation or relying on platform-specific RNG jump-ahead.
+        slab.resize(static_cast<size_t>(a.block) * a.n);
+        packed.resize(a.area);
+    }
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < a.blocks; ++i) {
+        const int rows = a.extent(i);
+        if (a.grid.rank == 0)
+            for (size_t t = 0; t < static_cast<size_t>(rows) * a.n; ++t)
+                slab[t] = rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < a.blocks; ++j) {
+            const int owner = a.grid.owner(i, j);
+            if (a.grid.rank == 0) {
+                std::fill(packed.begin(), packed.end(), 0.0);
+                const int cols = a.extent(j);
+                for (int r = 0; r < rows; ++r)
+                    std::copy_n(slab.data() + r * a.n + j * a.block, cols,
+                                packed.data() + r * a.block);
+                if (owner == 0)
+                    std::copy(packed.begin(), packed.end(), random.tile(i, j));
+                else
+                    MPI_Send(packed.data(), static_cast<int>(a.area), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                             owner, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            } else if (a.grid.rank == owner) {
+                MPI_Recv(random.tile(i, j), static_cast<int>(a.area), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         0, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < a.blocks; ++k) {
+        panels.exchange(random, k, 0);
+        for (const Tile& tile : a.tiles)
+            tileProduct<true>(a.data.data() + tile.offset,
+                              panels.left.data() + (tile.i / a.grid.rows) * a.area,
+                              panels.right.data() + panels.offsets[tile.j], a.block);
+    }
+    for (const Tile& tile : a.tiles) {
+        if (tile.i == tile.j) {
+            double* diagonal = a.data.data() + tile.offset;
+            for (int i = 0; i < a.extent(tile.i); ++i)
+                diagonal[i * a.block + i] += a.n;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(DistributedMatrix& a, PanelWorkspace& panels) {
+    std::vector<double> diagonal(a.area + 1);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < a.blocks; ++k) {
+        const int width = a.extent(k);
+        const int owner = a.grid.owner(k, k);
+        if (a.grid.rank == owner) {
+            double* d = a.tile(k, k);
+            diagonal[a.area] = 0.0;
+            for (int i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+                for (int j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
+                    double sum = 0.0;
+                    for (int t = 0; t < j; ++t)
+                        sum += d[i * a.block + t] * d[j * a.block + t];
+                    const double value = d[i * a.block + j] - sum;
+                    if (i == j) {
+                        if (!(value > 0.0) || !std::isfinite(value)) {
+                            diagonal[a.area] = static_cast<double>(k * a.block + i + 1);
+                            break;
+                        }
+                        d[i * a.block + j] = std::sqrt(value);
+                    } else {
+                        d[i * a.block + j] = value / d[j * a.block + j];
+                    }
                 }
-                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
-            } else {
-                // Off-diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
+                if (diagonal[a.area] != 0.0)
+                    break;
+                std::fill(d + i * a.block + i + 1, d + (i + 1) * a.block, 0.0);
+            }
+            std::copy_n(d, a.area, diagonal.data());
+        }
+        // Include the failure flag in the diagonal broadcast so every rank
+        // takes the same exit path, without an extra reduction per panel.
+        MPI_Bcast(diagonal.data(), static_cast<int>(a.area + 1), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (diagonal[a.area] != 0.0) {
+            if (a.grid.rank == 0)
+                printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n",
+                       static_cast<size_t>(diagonal[a.area] - 1));
+            return false;
+        }
+        if (k + 1 == a.blocks)
+            continue;
+        if (a.grid.col == static_cast<int>(k % a.grid.cols)) {
+            for (const Tile& tile : a.tiles) {
+                if (tile.j != k || tile.i <= k)
+                    continue;
+                double* panel = a.data.data() + tile.offset;
+                for (int i = 0; i < a.extent(tile.i); ++i) {
+                    for (int j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+                        double sum = 0.0;
+                        for (int t = 0; t < j; ++t)
+                            sum += panel[i * a.block + t] * diagonal[j * a.block + t];
+                        panel[i * a.block + j] =
+                            (panel[i * a.block + j] - sum) / diagonal[j * a.block + j];
+                    }
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
             }
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+        panels.exchange(a, k, k + 1);
+        for (const Tile& tile : a.tiles) {
+            if (tile.j > k)
+                tileProduct<false>(a.data.data() + tile.offset,
+                                   panels.left.data() + (tile.i / a.grid.rows) * a.area,
+                                   panels.right.data() + panels.offsets[tile.j], a.block);
         }
     }
-    
     return true;
 }

+bool validateCholesky(const DistributedMatrix& a, const std::vector<double>& original,

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+struct ProcessGrid {
+    int rank, size, rows, cols, row, col;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm, colComm;
+
+    ProcessGrid() {
+        MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);
+        int dims[2] = {0, 0};
+        MPI_Dims_create(size, 2, dims);
+        rows = dims[0];
+        cols = dims[1];
+        row = rank / cols;
+        col = rank % cols;
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, row, col, &rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, col, row, &colComm);
+    }
+
+    ~ProcessGrid() {
+        MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&colComm);
+    }
+
+    int owner(size_t i, size_t j) const {
+        return static_cast<int>(i % rows) * cols + static_cast<int>(j % cols);
+    }
+};
+
+struct Tile {
+    size_t i, j, offset;
+};
+
+// Only locally owned tiles are allocated. The factor stores the lower half;
+// the temporary random matrix uses the same layout with all tiles present.
+struct DistributedMatrix {
+    const ProcessGrid& grid;
+    size_t n, blocks, localRows, localCols, area;
+    int block;
+    std::vector<size_t> offsets;
+    std::vector<Tile> tiles;
+    std::vector<double> data;
+
+    DistributedMatrix(const ProcessGrid& g, size_t size, int b, bool lower = true)
+        : grid(g), n(size), blocks(size / b + (size % b != 0)),
+          localRows(blocks / g.rows + (static_cast<size_t>(g.row) < blocks % g.rows)),
+          localCols(blocks / g.cols + (static_cast<size_t>(g.col) < blocks % g.cols)),
+          area(static_cast<size_t>(b) * b), block(b),
+          offsets(localRows * localCols, std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
+        // Panel collectives use MPI's int counts; the matrix itself may exceed
+        // this limit and is transferred in chunks when results are requested.
+        if (std::max(localRows, localCols) > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) / area)
+            throw std::length_error("Matrix panels exceed the MPI count limit");
+        for (size_t i = g.row; i < blocks; i += g.rows) {
+            for (size_t j = g.col; j < blocks && (!lower || j <= i); j += g.cols) {
+                const size_t offset = tiles.size() * area;
+                offsets[(i / g.rows) * localCols + j / g.cols] = offset;
+                tiles.push_back({i, j, offset});
+            }
+        }
+        data.resize(tiles.size() * area, 0.0);
+    }
+
+    const double* tile(size_t i, size_t j) const {
+        const size_t offset = offsets[(i / grid.rows) * localCols + j / grid.cols];
+        return offset == std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() ? nullptr : data.data() + offset;
+    }
+
+    double* tile(size_t i, size_t j) {
+        return const_cast<double*>(static_cast<const DistributedMatrix&>(*this).tile(i, j));
+    }
+
+    int extent(size_t i) const {
+        return static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(block), n - i * block));
+    }
+};
+
+struct PanelWorkspace {
+    std::vector<double> left, right;
+    std::vector<int> counts, displacements;
+    std::vector<size_t> offsets;
+
+    explicit PanelWorkspace(const DistributedMatrix& a)
+        : left(a.localRows * a.area), right(a.localCols * a.area),
+          counts(a.grid.rows), displacements(a.grid.rows), offsets(a.blocks) {}
 
-bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
-    // A is stored in row-major order
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            
-            if (i == j) {
-                // Diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
+    // Broadcast the column panel along grid rows, then redistribute its
+    // transpose within grid columns. Aggregating tiles gives two collectives
+    // per panel, including on nonsquare process grids. The communicated volume
+    // per rank is O(n * block / sqrt(p)) on a balanced grid.
+    void exchange(const DistributedMatrix& a, size_t k, size_t first) {
+        const ProcessGrid& g = a.grid;
+        const size_t firstRow = first / g.rows + (static_cast<size_t>(g.row) < first % g.rows);
+        if (g.col == static_cast<int>(k % g.cols)) {
+            for (size_t r = firstRow; r < a.localRows; ++r) {
+                const double* source = a.tile(r * g.rows + g.row, k);
+                double* target = left.data() + r * a.area;
+                if (source)
+                    std::copy_n(source, a.area, target);
+                else
+                    std::fill_n(target, a.area, 0.0);
+            }
+        }
+        const int count = static_cast<int>((a.localRows - firstRow) * a.area);
+        MPI_Bcast(count ? left.data() + firstRow * a.area : left.data(), count,
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, static_cast<int>(k % g.cols), g.rowComm);
+
+        std::fill(counts.begin(), counts.end(), 0);
+        const size_t firstCol = first / g.cols + (static_cast<size_t>(g.col) < first % g.cols);
+        for (size_t c = firstCol; c < a.localCols; ++c) {
+            const size_t j = c * g.cols + g.col;
+            offsets[j] = counts[j % g.rows];
+            counts[j % g.rows] += static_cast<int>(a.area);
+        }
+        int total = 0;
+        for (int r = 0; r < g.rows; ++r) {
+            displacements[r] = total;
+            total += counts[r];
+        }
+        for (size_t c = firstCol; c < a.localCols; ++c) {
+            const size_t j = c * g.cols + g.col;
+            offsets[j] += displacements[j % g.rows];
+            if (static_cast<int>(j % g.rows) == g.row) {
+                const double* source = left.data() + (j / g.rows) * a.area;
+                double* target = right.data() + offsets[j];
+                for (int i = 0; i < a.block; ++i)
+                    for (int t = 0; t < a.block; ++t)
+                        target[t * a.block + i] = source[i * a.block + t];
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DOUBLE, right.data(), counts.data(),
+                       displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, g.colComm);
+    }
+};

+// A portable 4-by-8 register kernel. SIMD runs across independent output
+// entries, avoiding horizontal reductions or unsafe floating-point flags.
+// add=true preserves the original k-order when generating/reconstructing A.
+template <bool add>
+void tileProduct(double* __restrict target, const double* __restrict left,
+                 const double* __restrict right, int block) {
+    for (int i = 0; i < block; i += 4) {
+        for (int j = 0; j < block; j += 8) {
+            double c0[8], c1[8], c2[8], c3[8];
+            for (int t = 0; t < 8; ++t) {
+                c0[t] = add ? target[(i + 0) * block + j + t] : 0.0;
+                c1[t] = add ? target[(i + 1) * block + j + t] : 0.0;
+                c2[t] = add ? target[(i + 2) * block + j + t] : 0.0;
+                c3[t] = add ? target[(i + 3) * block + j + t] : 0.0;
+            }
+            for (int k = 0; k < block; ++k) {
+                const double a0 = left[(i + 0) * block + k];
+                const double a1 = left[(i + 1) * block + k];
+                const double a2 = left[(i + 2) * block + k];
+                const double a3 = left[(i + 3) * block + k];
+                const double* b = right + k * block + j;
+                for (int t = 0; t < 8; ++t) {
+                    c0[t] += a0 * b[t];
+                    c1[t] += a1 * b[t];
+                    c2[t] += a2 * b[t];
+                    c3[t] += a3 * b[t];
                 }
-                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
-                if (val <= 0.0) {
-                    // Matrix is not positive definite
-                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
-                    return false;
+            }
+            for (int t = 0; t < 8; ++t) {
+                if constexpr (add) {
+                    target[(i + 0) * block + j + t] = c0[t];
+                    target[(i + 1) * block + j + t] = c1[t];
+                    target[(i + 2) * block + j + t] = c2[t];
+                    target[(i + 3) * block + j + t] = c3[t];
+                } else {
+                    target[(i + 0) * block + j + t] -= c0[t];
+                    target[(i + 1) * block + j + t] -= c1[t];
+                    target[(i + 2) * block + j + t] -= c2[t];
+                    target[(i + 3) * block + j + t] -= c3[t];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(DistributedMatrix& a, PanelWorkspace& panels) {
+    DistributedMatrix random(a.grid, a.n, a.block, false);
+    std::vector<double> slab, packed;
+    if (a.grid.rank == 0) {
+        // Stream the exact original rand_r sequence without a replicated n^2
+        // input allocation or relying on platform-specific RNG jump-ahead.
+        slab.resize(static_cast<size_t>(a.block) * a.n);
+        packed.resize(a.area);
+    }
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < a.blocks; ++i) {
+        const int rows = a.extent(i);
+        if (a.grid.rank == 0)
+            for (size_t t = 0; t < static_cast<size_t>(rows) * a.n; ++t)
+                slab[t] = rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < a.blocks; ++j) {
+            const int owner = a.grid.owner(i, j);
+            if (a.grid.rank == 0) {
+                std::fill(packed.begin(), packed.end(), 0.0);
+                const int cols = a.extent(j);
+                for (int r = 0; r < rows; ++r)
+                    std::copy_n(slab.data() + r * a.n + j * a.block, cols,
+                                packed.data() + r * a.block);
+                if (owner == 0)
+                    std::copy(packed.begin(), packed.end(), random.tile(i, j));
+                else
+                    MPI_Send(packed.data(), static_cast<int>(a.area), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                             owner, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            } else if (a.grid.rank == owner) {
+                MPI_Recv(random.tile(i, j), static_cast<int>(a.area), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         0, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < a.blocks; ++k) {
+        panels.exchange(random, k, 0);
+        for (const Tile& tile : a.tiles)
+            tileProduct<true>(a.data.data() + tile.offset,
+                              panels.left.data() + (tile.i / a.grid.rows) * a.area,
+                              panels.right.data() + panels.offsets[tile.j], a.block);
+    }
+    for (const Tile& tile : a.tiles) {
+        if (tile.i == tile.j) {
+            double* diagonal = a.data.data() + tile.offset;
+            for (int i = 0; i < a.extent(tile.i); ++i)
+                diagonal[i * a.block + i] += a.n;
+        }
+    }
+}

+bool choleskyDecomposition(DistributedMatrix& a, PanelWorkspace& panels) {
+    std::vector<double> diagonal(a.area + 1);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < a.blocks; ++k) {
+        const int width = a.extent(k);
+        const int owner = a.grid.owner(k, k);
+        if (a.grid.rank == owner) {
+            double* d = a.tile(k, k);
+            diagonal[a.area] = 0.0;
+            for (int i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+                for (int j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
+                    double sum = 0.0;
+                    for (int t = 0; t < j; ++t)
+                        sum += d[i * a.block + t] * d[j * a.block + t];
+                    const double value = d[i * a.block + j] - sum;
+                    if (i == j) {
+                        if (!(value > 0.0) || !std::isfinite(value)) {
+                            diagonal[a.area] = static_cast<double>(k * a.block + i + 1);
+                            break;
+                        }
+                        d[i * a.block + j] = std::sqrt(value);
+                    } else {
+                        d[i * a.block + j] = value / d[j * a.block + j];
+                    }
                 }
-                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
-            } else {
-                // Off-diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
+                if (diagonal[a.area] != 0.0)
+                    break;
+                std::fill(d + i * a.block + i + 1, d + (i + 1) * a.block, 0.0);
+            }
+            std::copy_n(d, a.area, diagonal.data());
+        }
+        // Include the failure flag in the diagonal broadcast so every rank
+        // takes the same exit path, without an extra reduction per panel.
+        MPI_Bcast(diagonal.data(), static_cast<int>(a.area + 1), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (diagonal[a.area] != 0.0) {
+            if (a.grid.rank == 0)
+                printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n",
+                       static_cast<size_t>(diagonal[a.area] - 1));
+            return false;
+        }
+        if (k + 1 == a.blocks)
+            continue;
+        if (a.grid.col == static_cast<int>(k % a.grid.cols)) {
+            for (const Tile& tile : a.tiles) {
+                if (tile.j != k || tile.i <= k)
+                    continue;
+                double* panel = a.data.data() + tile.offset;
+                for (int i = 0; i < a.extent(tile.i); ++i) {
+                    for (int j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+                        double sum = 0.0;
+                        for (int t = 0; t < j; ++t)
+                            sum += panel[i * a.block + t] * diagonal[j * a.block + t];
+                        panel[i * a.block + j] =
+                            (panel[i * a.block + j] - sum) / diagonal[j * a.block + j];
+                    }
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
             }
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+        panels.exchange(a, k, k + 1);
+        for (const Tile& tile : a.tiles) {
+            if (tile.j > k)
+                tileProduct<false>(a.data.data() + tile.offset,
+                                   panels.left.data() + (tile.i / a.grid.rows) * a.area,
+                                   panels.right.data() + panels.offsets[tile.j], a.block);
         }
     }
-    
     return true;

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+struct ProcessGrid {
+    int rank, size, rows, cols, row, col;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm, colComm;
+
+    ProcessGrid() {
+        MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);
+        int dims[2] = {0, 0};
+        MPI_Dims_create(size, 2, dims);
+        rows = dims[0];
+        cols = dims[1];
+        row = rank / cols;
+        col = rank % cols;
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, row, col, &rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, col, row, &colComm);
+    }
+
+    ~ProcessGrid() {
+        MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&colComm);
+    }
+
+    int owner(size_t i, size_t j) const {
+        return static_cast<int>(i % rows) * cols + static_cast<int>(j % cols);
+    }
+};
+
+struct Tile {
+    size_t i, j, offset;
+};
+
+// Only locally owned tiles are allocated. The factor stores the lower half;
+// the temporary random matrix uses the same layout with all tiles present.
+struct DistributedMatrix {
+    const ProcessGrid& grid;
+    size_t n, blocks, localRows, localCols, area;
+    int block;
+    std::vector<size_t> offsets;
+    std::vector<Tile> tiles;
+    std::vector<double> data;
+
+    DistributedMatrix(const ProcessGrid& g, size_t size, int b, bool lower = true)
+        : grid(g), n(size), blocks(size / b + (size % b != 0)),
+          localRows(blocks / g.rows + (static_cast<size_t>(g.row) < blocks % g.rows)),
+          localCols(blocks / g.cols + (static_cast<size_t>(g.col) < blocks % g.cols)),
+          area(static_cast<size_t>(b) * b), block(b),
+          offsets(localRows * localCols, std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
+        // Panel collectives use MPI's int counts; the matrix itself may exceed
+        // this limit and is transferred in chunks when results are requested.
+        if (std::max(localRows, localCols) > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) / area)
+            throw std::length_error("Matrix panels exceed the MPI count limit");
+        for (size_t i = g.row; i < blocks; i += g.rows) {
+            for (size_t j = g.col; j < blocks && (!lower || j <= i); j += g.cols) {
+                const size_t offset = tiles.size() * area;
+                offsets[(i / g.rows) * localCols + j / g.cols] = offset;
+                tiles.push_back({i, j, offset});
+            }
+        }
+        data.resize(tiles.size() * area, 0.0);
+    }
+
+    const double* tile(size_t i, size_t j) const {
+        const size_t offset = offsets[(i / grid.rows) * localCols + j / grid.cols];
+        return offset == std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() ? nullptr : data.data() + offset;
+    }
+
+    double* tile(size_t i, size_t j) {
+        return const_cast<double*>(static_cast<const DistributedMatrix&>(*this).tile(i, j));
+    }
+
+    int extent(size_t i) const {
+        return static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(block), n - i * block));
+    }
+};

+struct PanelWorkspace {
+    std::vector<double> left, right;
+    std::vector<int> counts, displacements;
+    std::vector<size_t> offsets;
+
+    explicit PanelWorkspace(const DistributedMatrix& a)
+        : left(a.localRows * a.area), right(a.localCols * a.area),
+          counts(a.grid.rows), displacements(a.grid.rows), offsets(a.blocks) {}
 
-bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
-    // A is stored in row-major order
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            
-            if (i == j) {
-                // Diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
+    // Broadcast the column panel along grid rows, then redistribute its
+    // transpose within grid columns. Aggregating tiles gives two collectives
+    // per panel, including on nonsquare process grids. The communicated volume
+    // per rank is O(n * block / sqrt(p)) on a balanced grid.
+    void exchange(const DistributedMatrix& a, size_t k, size_t first) {
+        const ProcessGrid& g = a.grid;
+        const size_t firstRow = first / g.rows + (static_cast<size_t>(g.row) < first % g.rows);
+        if (g.col == static_cast<int>(k % g.cols)) {
+            for (size_t r = firstRow; r < a.localRows; ++r) {
+                const double* source = a.tile(r * g.rows + g.row, k);
+                double* target = left.data() + r * a.area;
+                if (source)
+                    std::copy_n(source, a.area, target);
+                else
+                    std::fill_n(target, a.area, 0.0);
+            }
+        }
+        const int count = static_cast<int>((a.localRows - firstRow) * a.area);
+        MPI_Bcast(count ? left.data() + firstRow * a.area : left.data(), count,
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, static_cast<int>(k % g.cols), g.rowComm);
+
+        std::fill(counts.begin(), counts.end(), 0);
+        const size_t firstCol = first / g.cols + (static_cast<size_t>(g.col) < first % g.cols);
+        for (size_t c = firstCol; c < a.localCols; ++c) {
+            const size_t j = c * g.cols + g.col;
+            offsets[j] = counts[j % g.rows];
+            counts[j % g.rows] += static_cast<int>(a.area);
+        }
+        int total = 0;
+        for (int r = 0; r < g.rows; ++r) {
+            displacements[r] = total;
+            total += counts[r];
+        }
+        for (size_t c = firstCol; c < a.localCols; ++c) {
+            const size_t j = c * g.cols + g.col;
+            offsets[j] += displacements[j % g.rows];
+            if (static_cast<int>(j % g.rows) == g.row) {
+                const double* source = left.data() + (j / g.rows) * a.area;
+                double* target = right.data() + offsets[j];
+                for (int i = 0; i < a.block; ++i)
+                    for (int t = 0; t < a.block; ++t)
+                        target[t * a.block + i] = source[i * a.block + t];
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DOUBLE, right.data(), counts.data(),
+                       displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, g.colComm);
+    }
+};
+
+// A portable 4-by-8 register kernel. SIMD runs across independent output
+// entries, avoiding horizontal reductions or unsafe floating-point flags.
+// add=true preserves the original k-order when generating/reconstructing A.
+template <bool add>
+void tileProduct(double* __restrict target, const double* __restrict left,
+                 const double* __restrict right, int block) {
+    for (int i = 0; i < block; i += 4) {
+        for (int j = 0; j < block; j += 8) {
+            double c0[8], c1[8], c2[8], c3[8];
+            for (int t = 0; t < 8; ++t) {
+                c0[t] = add ? target[(i + 0) * block + j + t] : 0.0;
+                c1[t] = add ? target[(i + 1) * block + j + t] : 0.0;
+                c2[t] = add ? target[(i + 2) * block + j + t] : 0.0;
+                c3[t] = add ? target[(i + 3) * block + j + t] : 0.0;
+            }
+            for (int k = 0; k < block; ++k) {
+                const double a0 = left[(i + 0) * block + k];
+                const double a1 = left[(i + 1) * block + k];
+                const double a2 = left[(i + 2) * block + k];
+                const double a3 = left[(i + 3) * block + k];
+                const double* b = right + k * block + j;
+                for (int t = 0; t < 8; ++t) {
+                    c0[t] += a0 * b[t];
+                    c1[t] += a1 * b[t];
+                    c2[t] += a2 * b[t];
+                    c3[t] += a3 * b[t];
                 }
-                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
-                if (val <= 0.0) {
-                    // Matrix is not positive definite
-                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
-                    return false;
+            }
+            for (int t = 0; t < 8; ++t) {
+                if constexpr (add) {
+                    target[(i + 0) * block + j + t] = c0[t];
+                    target[(i + 1) * block + j + t] = c1[t];
+                    target[(i + 2) * block + j + t] = c2[t];
+                    target[(i + 3) * block + j + t] = c3[t];
+                } else {
+                    target[(i + 0) * block + j + t] -= c0[t];
+                    target[(i + 1) * block + j + t] -= c1[t];
+                    target[(i + 2) * block + j + t] -= c2[t];
+                    target[(i + 3) * block + j + t] -= c3[t];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(DistributedMatrix& a, PanelWorkspace& panels) {
+    DistributedMatrix random(a.grid, a.n, a.block, false);
+    std::vector<double> slab, packed;
+    if (a.grid.rank == 0) {
+        // Stream the exact original rand_r sequence without a replicated n^2
+        // input allocation or relying on platform-specific RNG jump-ahead.
+        slab.resize(static_cast<size_t>(a.block) * a.n);
+        packed.resize(a.area);
+    }
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < a.blocks; ++i) {
+        const int rows = a.extent(i);
+        if (a.grid.rank == 0)
+            for (size_t t = 0; t < static_cast<size_t>(rows) * a.n; ++t)
+                slab[t] = rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < a.blocks; ++j) {
+            const int owner = a.grid.owner(i, j);
+            if (a.grid.rank == 0) {
+                std::fill(packed.begin(), packed.end(), 0.0);
+                const int cols = a.extent(j);
+                for (int r = 0; r < rows; ++r)
+                    std::copy_n(slab.data() + r * a.n + j * a.block, cols,
+                                packed.data() + r * a.block);
+                if (owner == 0)
+                    std::copy(packed.begin(), packed.end(), random.tile(i, j));
+                else
+                    MPI_Send(packed.data(), static_cast<int>(a.area), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                             owner, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            } else if (a.grid.rank == owner) {
+                MPI_Recv(random.tile(i, j), static_cast<int>(a.area), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         0, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < a.blocks; ++k) {
+        panels.exchange(random, k, 0);
+        for (const Tile& tile : a.tiles)
+            tileProduct<true>(a.data.data() + tile.offset,
+                              panels.left.data() + (tile.i / a.grid.rows) * a.area,
+                              panels.right.data() + panels.offsets[tile.j], a.block);
+    }
+    for (const Tile& tile : a.tiles) {
+        if (tile.i == tile.j) {
+            double* diagonal = a.data.data() + tile.offset;
+            for (int i = 0; i < a.extent(tile.i); ++i)
+                diagonal[i * a.block + i] += a.n;
+        }
+    }
+}

+bool choleskyDecomposition(DistributedMatrix& a, PanelWorkspace& panels) {
+    std::vector<double> diagonal(a.area + 1);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < a.blocks; ++k) {
+        const int width = a.extent(k);
+        const int owner = a.grid.owner(k, k);
+        if (a.grid.rank == owner) {
+            double* d = a.tile(k, k);
+            diagonal[a.area] = 0.0;
+            for (int i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+                for (int j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
+                    double sum = 0.0;
+                    for (int t = 0; t < j; ++t)
+                        sum += d[i * a.block + t] * d[j * a.block + t];
+                    const double value = d[i * a.block + j] - sum;
+                    if (i == j) {
+                        if (!(value > 0.0) || !std::isfinite(value)) {
+                            diagonal[a.area] = static_cast<double>(k * a.block + i + 1);
+                            break;
+                        }
+                        d[i * a.block + j] = std::sqrt(value);
+                    } else {
+                        d[i * a.block + j] = value / d[j * a.block + j];
+                    }
                 }
-                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
-            } else {
-                // Off-diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
+                if (diagonal[a.area] != 0.0)
+                    break;
+                std::fill(d + i * a.block + i + 1, d + (i + 1) * a.block, 0.0);
+            }
+            std::copy_n(d, a.area, diagonal.data());
+        }
+        // Include the failure flag in the diagonal broadcast so every rank
+        // takes the same exit path, without an extra reduction per panel.
+        MPI_Bcast(diagonal.data(), static_cast<int>(a.area + 1), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (diagonal[a.area] != 0.0) {
+            if (a.grid.rank == 0)
+                printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n",
+                       static_cast<size_t>(diagonal[a.area] - 1));
+            return false;
+        }
+        if (k + 1 == a.blocks)
+            continue;
+        if (a.grid.col == static_cast<int>(k % a.grid.cols)) {
+            for (const Tile& tile : a.tiles) {
+                if (tile.j != k || tile.i <= k)
+                    continue;
+                double* panel = a.data.data() + tile.offset;
+                for (int i = 0; i < a.extent(tile.i); ++i) {
+                    for (int j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+                        double sum = 0.0;
+                        for (int t = 0; t < j; ++t)
+                            sum += panel[i * a.block + t] * diagonal[j * a.block + t];
+                        panel[i * a.block + j] =
+                            (panel[i * a.block + j] - sum) / diagonal[j * a.block + j];
+                    }
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
             }
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+        panels.exchange(a, k, k + 1);
+        for (const Tile& tile : a.tiles) {
+            if (tile.j > k)
+                tileProduct<false>(a.data.data() + tile.offset,
+                                   panels.left.data() + (tile.i / a.grid.rows) * a.area,
+                                   panels.right.data() + panels.offsets[tile.j], a.block);
         }
     }
-    
     return true;
 }

+struct ProcessGrid {
+    int rank, size, rows, cols, row, col;
+    MPI_Comm rowComm, colComm;
+
+    ProcessGrid() {
+        MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);
+        int dims[2] = {0, 0};
+        MPI_Dims_create(size, 2, dims);
+        rows = dims[0];
+        cols = dims[1];
+        row = rank / cols;
+        col = rank % cols;
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, row, col, &rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, col, row, &colComm);
+    }
+
+    ~ProcessGrid() {
+        MPI_Comm_free(&rowComm);
+        MPI_Comm_free(&colComm);
+    }
+
+    int owner(size_t i, size_t j) const {
+        return static_cast<int>(i % rows) * cols + static_cast<int>(j % cols);
+    }
+};
+
+struct Tile {
+    size_t i, j, offset;
+};
+
+// Only locally owned tiles are allocated. The factor stores the lower half;
+// the temporary random matrix uses the same layout with all tiles present.
+struct DistributedMatrix {
+    const ProcessGrid& grid;
+    size_t n, blocks, localRows, localCols, area;
+    int block;
+    std::vector<size_t> offsets;
+    std::vector<Tile> tiles;
+    std::vector<double> data;
+
+    DistributedMatrix(const ProcessGrid& g, size_t size, int b, bool lower = true)
+        : grid(g), n(size), blocks(size / b + (size % b != 0)),
+          localRows(blocks / g.rows + (static_cast<size_t>(g.row) < blocks % g.rows)),
+          localCols(blocks / g.cols + (static_cast<size_t>(g.col) < blocks % g.cols)),
+          area(static_cast<size_t>(b) * b), block(b),
+          offsets(localRows * localCols, std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
+        // Panel collectives use MPI's int counts; the matrix itself may exceed
+        // this limit and is transferred in chunks when results are requested.
+        if (std::max(localRows, localCols) > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) / area)
+            throw std::length_error("Matrix panels exceed the MPI count limit");
+        for (size_t i = g.row; i < blocks; i += g.rows) {
+            for (size_t j = g.col; j < blocks && (!lower || j <= i); j += g.cols) {
+                const size_t offset = tiles.size() * area;
+                offsets[(i / g.rows) * localCols + j / g.cols] = offset;
+                tiles.push_back({i, j, offset});
+            }
+        }
+        data.resize(tiles.size() * area, 0.0);
+    }
+
+    const double* tile(size_t i, size_t j) const {
+        const size_t offset = offsets[(i / grid.rows) * localCols + j / grid.cols];
+        return offset == std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() ? nullptr : data.data() + offset;
+    }
+
+    double* tile(size_t i, size_t j) {
+        return const_cast<double*>(static_cast<const DistributedMatrix&>(*this).tile(i, j));
+    }
+
+    int extent(size_t i) const {
+        return static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(block), n - i * block));
+    }
+};
+
+struct PanelWorkspace {
+    std::vector<double> left, right;
+    std::vector<int> counts, displacements;
+    std::vector<size_t> offsets;
+
+    explicit PanelWorkspace(const DistributedMatrix& a)
+        : left(a.localRows * a.area), right(a.localCols * a.area),
+          counts(a.grid.rows), displacements(a.grid.rows), offsets(a.blocks) {}

+    // transpose within grid columns. Aggregating tiles gives two collectives
+    // per panel, including on nonsquare process grids. The communicated volume
+    // per rank is O(n * block / sqrt(p)) on a balanced grid.
+    void exchange(const DistributedMatrix& a, size_t k, size_t first) {
+        const ProcessGrid& g = a.grid;
+        const size_t firstRow = first / g.rows + (static_cast<size_t>(g.row) < first % g.rows);
+        if (g.col == static_cast<int>(k % g.cols)) {
+            for (size_t r = firstRow; r < a.localRows; ++r) {
+                const double* source = a.tile(r * g.rows + g.row, k);
+                double* target = left.data() + r * a.area;
+                if (source)
+                    std::copy_n(source, a.area, target);
+                else
+                    std::fill_n(target, a.area, 0.0);
+            }
+        }
+        const int count = static_cast<int>((a.localRows - firstRow) * a.area);
+        MPI_Bcast(count ? left.data() + firstRow * a.area : left.data(), count,
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, static_cast<int>(k % g.cols), g.rowComm);
+
+        std::fill(counts.begin(), counts.end(), 0);
+        const size_t firstCol = first / g.cols + (static_cast<size_t>(g.col) < first % g.cols);
+        for (size_t c = firstCol; c < a.localCols; ++c) {
+            const size_t j = c * g.cols + g.col;
+            offsets[j] = counts[j % g.rows];
+            counts[j % g.rows] += static_cast<int>(a.area);
+        }
+        int total = 0;
+        for (int r = 0; r < g.rows; ++r) {
+            displacements[r] = total;
+            total += counts[r];
+        }
+        for (size_t c = firstCol; c < a.localCols; ++c) {
+            const size_t j = c * g.cols + g.col;
+            offsets[j] += displacements[j % g.rows];
+            if (static_cast<int>(j % g.rows) == g.row) {
+                const double* source = left.data() + (j / g.rows) * a.area;
+                double* target = right.data() + offsets[j];
+                for (int i = 0; i < a.block; ++i)
+                    for (int t = 0; t < a.block; ++t)
+                        target[t * a.block + i] = source[i * a.block + t];
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DOUBLE, right.data(), counts.data(),
+                       displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, g.colComm);
+    }
+};

+// A portable 4-by-8 register kernel. SIMD runs across independent output
+// entries, avoiding horizontal reductions or unsafe floating-point flags.
+// add=true preserves the original k-order when generating/reconstructing A.
+template <bool add>
+void tileProduct(double* __restrict target, const double* __restrict left,
+                 const double* __restrict right, int block) {
+    for (int i = 0; i < block; i += 4) {
+        for (int j = 0; j < block; j += 8) {
+            double c0[8], c1[8], c2[8], c3[8];
+            for (int t = 0; t < 8; ++t) {
+                c0[t] = add ? target[(i + 0) * block + j + t] : 0.0;
+                c1[t] = add ? target[(i + 1) * block + j + t] : 0.0;
+                c2[t] = add ? target[(i + 2) * block + j + t] : 0.0;
+                c3[t] = add ? target[(i + 3) * block + j + t] : 0.0;
+            }
+            for (int k = 0; k < block; ++k) {
+                const double a0 = left[(i + 0) * block + k];
+                const double a1 = left[(i + 1) * block + k];
+                const double a2 = left[(i + 2) * block + k];
+                const double a3 = left[(i + 3) * block + k];
+                const double* b = right + k * block + j;
+                for (int t = 0; t < 8; ++t) {
+                    c0[t] += a0 * b[t];
+                    c1[t] += a1 * b[t];
+                    c2[t] += a2 * b[t];
+                    c3[t] += a3 * b[t];
                 }
-                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
-                if (val <= 0.0) {
-                    // Matrix is not positive definite
-                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
-                    return false;
+            }
+            for (int t = 0; t < 8; ++t) {
+                if constexpr (add) {
+                    target[(i + 0) * block + j + t] = c0[t];
+                    target[(i + 1) * block + j + t] = c1[t];
+                    target[(i + 2) * block + j + t] = c2[t];
+                    target[(i + 3) * block + j + t] = c3[t];
+                } else {
+                    target[(i + 0) * block + j + t] -= c0[t];
+                    target[(i + 1) * block + j + t] -= c1[t];
+                    target[(i + 2) * block + j + t] -= c2[t];
+                    target[(i + 3) * block + j + t] -= c3[t];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }

+void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(DistributedMatrix& a, PanelWorkspace& panels) {
+    DistributedMatrix random(a.grid, a.n, a.block, false);
+    std::vector<double> slab, packed;
+    if (a.grid.rank == 0) {
+        // Stream the exact original rand_r sequence without a replicated n^2
+        // input allocation or relying on platform-specific RNG jump-ahead.
+        slab.resize(static_cast<size_t>(a.block) * a.n);
+        packed.resize(a.area);
+    }
+    unsigned int seed = 42;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < a.blocks; ++i) {
+        const int rows = a.extent(i);
+        if (a.grid.rank == 0)
+            for (size_t t = 0; t < static_cast<size_t>(rows) * a.n; ++t)
+                slab[t] = rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < a.blocks; ++j) {
+            const int owner = a.grid.owner(i, j);
+            if (a.grid.rank == 0) {
+                std::fill(packed.begin(), packed.end(), 0.0);
+                const int cols = a.extent(j);
+                for (int r = 0; r < rows; ++r)
+                    std::copy_n(slab.data() + r * a.n + j * a.block, cols,
+                                packed.data() + r * a.block);
+                if (owner == 0)
+                    std::copy(packed.begin(), packed.end(), random.tile(i, j));
+                else
+                    MPI_Send(packed.data(), static_cast<int>(a.area), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                             owner, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            } else if (a.grid.rank == owner) {
+                MPI_Recv(random.tile(i, j), static_cast<int>(a.area), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         0, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < a.blocks; ++k) {
+        panels.exchange(random, k, 0);
+        for (const Tile& tile : a.tiles)
+            tileProduct<true>(a.data.data() + tile.offset,
+                              panels.left.data() + (tile.i / a.grid.rows) * a.area,
+                              panels.right.data() + panels.offsets[tile.j], a.block);
+    }
+    for (const Tile& tile : a.tiles) {
+        if (tile.i == tile.j) {
+            double* diagonal = a.data.data() + tile.offset;
+            for (int i = 0; i < a.extent(tile.i); ++i)
+                diagonal[i * a.block + i] += a.n;
+        }
+    }
+}

+bool choleskyDecomposition(DistributedMatrix& a, PanelWorkspace& panels) {
+    std::vector<double> diagonal(a.area + 1);
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < a.blocks; ++k) {
+        const int width = a.extent(k);
+        const int owner = a.grid.owner(k, k);
+        if (a.grid.rank == owner) {
+            double* d = a.tile(k, k);
+            diagonal[a.area] = 0.0;
+            for (int i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+                for (int j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
+                    double sum = 0.0;
+                    for (int t = 0; t < j; ++t)
+                        sum += d[i * a.block + t] * d[j * a.block + t];
+                    const double value = d[i * a.block + j] - sum;
+                    if (i == j) {
+                        if (!(value > 0.0) || !std::isfinite(value)) {
+                            diagonal[a.area] = static_cast<double>(k * a.block + i + 1);
+                            break;
+                        }
+                        d[i * a.block + j] = std::sqrt(value);
+                    } else {
+                        d[i * a.block + j] = value / d[j * a.block + j];
+                    }
                 }
-                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
-            } else {
-                // Off-diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
+                if (diagonal[a.area] != 0.0)
+                    break;
+                std::fill(d + i * a.block + i + 1, d + (i + 1) * a.block, 0.0);
+            }
+            std::copy_n(d, a.area, diagonal.data());
+        }
+        // Include the failure flag in the diagonal broadcast so every rank
+        // takes the same exit path, without an extra reduction per panel.
+        MPI_Bcast(diagonal.data(), static_cast<int>(a.area + 1), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (diagonal[a.area] != 0.0) {
+            if (a.grid.rank == 0)
+                printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n",
+                       static_cast<size_t>(diagonal[a.area] - 1));
+            return false;
+        }
+        if (k + 1 == a.blocks)
+            continue;
+        if (a.grid.col == static_cast<int>(k % a.grid.cols)) {
+            for (const Tile& tile : a.tiles) {
+                if (tile.j != k || tile.i <= k)
+                    continue;
+                double* panel = a.data.data() + tile.offset;
+                for (int i = 0; i < a.extent(tile.i); ++i) {
+                    for (int j = 0; j < width; ++j) {
+                        double sum = 0.0;
+                        for (int t = 0; t < j; ++t)
+                            sum += panel[i * a.block + t] * diagonal[j * a.block + t];
+                        panel[i * a.block + j] =
+                            (panel[i * a.block + j] - sum) / diagonal[j * a.block + j];
+                    }
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
             }
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+        panels.exchange(a, k, k + 1);
+        for (const Tile& tile : a.tiles) {
+            if (tile.j > k)
+                tileProduct<false>(a.data.data() + tile.offset,
+                                   panels.left.data() + (tile.i / a.grid.rows) * a.area,
+                                   panels.right.data() + panels.offsets[tile.j], a.block);
         }
     }
-    
     return true;
 }

codex
The MPI build passes validation on four ranks with a partial final tile. Generation and validation are distributed, and reported time uses the slowest rank’s elapsed time. I’m checking more process-grid shapes, edge sizes, and every factor entry against the original algorithm, then measuring performance.
